# CTF Writeups & Walkthroughs Central Repository

Welcome to the **CTF_Setup** central archive! This directory contains **1168** verified CTF writeup repositories with over **30,390** Markdown (`.md`) writeup files covering TryHackMe, HackTheBox, PicoCTF, elite team solutions, and major competitions.

## Summary by Category

| Category | Cloned Repositories | Total `.md` Writeup Files |
|:---|:---:|:---:|
| **Cryptography and Steganography** | 227 | 1,739 |
| **General CTFs and AllInOne** | 507 | 15,045 |
| **HackTheBox** | 78 | 1,368 |
| **PicoCTF** | 48 | 1,448 |
| **Top Players and Teams** | 110 | 6,338 |
| **TryHackMe** | 98 | 3,450 |
| **VulnHub and WebSecurity** | 100 | 1,002 |
| **Total** | **1168** | **30,390** |
